1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the devices space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 127 and the convergence rate is O(445/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 127 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the devices structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 445. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
